{
  "parentUuid": "265ec1b8-ed69-4301-8904-85705dd5f0f8",

    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

5. memory.md if needed

Current GPU status:
- cuda:0: reeval_flat_nli_s42 (just submitted, RUNNING)
- cuda:1: eval_rsft_ced_s43 (RUNNING)


- cuda:2: eval_rsft_ced_s45 (RUNNING)
- cuda:3: reeval_flat_nli_s43_r2 (RUNNING)

All 4 GPUs are fully utilized. The worker pool only has the analysis worker which has already completed, so I need to spin up at least 2 more workers to handle the queue. I'll prioritize building and sending the Director report first since the system will auto-update report.md, then check the literature map.

For the Director report, I'm pulling together the key findings: eval_plan_014_no_evidence_r2 succeeded with rel_f1=0.4876, which shows an evidence tax of 7.7 percentage points compared to the evidence-SFT baseline at 0.4106. This result validates C1 and the unified-protocol number matches our earlier intermediate eval at 0.4893, confirming reproducibility. The evidence tax is now solidly established at 7.7pp, and the CED reranker performance sits at 0.4864, which helps frame the next steps in the ablation analysis.

Before reporting to the Director, I'm also checking how the no-evidence SFT result compares against the RSFT baselines from our seed comparison notes. The no-evidence SFT at 0.4876 outperforms both the flat-NLI RSFT mean of 0.4559 and the CED RSFT mean of 0.4614 by roughly 3 and 2.6 percentage points respectively, which strengthens the narrative around the evidence generation cost.

But I need to be careful about the right baseline comparison—the no-evidence result should really be measured against the evidence-SFT baseline, not RSFT. Looking back at the roadmap, the evidence-SFT baseline sits at 0.4106, which would put the evidence tax at around 8 percentage points. I'm trying to reconcile whether the 0.4893 from the earlier plan was the same as our current 0.4876 unified-protocol result, and what exactly the 0.4106 evidence-SFT number represents.

Tracing through the numbers more carefully: the no-evidence approach (0.4876) versus evidence-SFT (0.4106) shows a 7.7pp gap—that's the evidence cost. But RSFT variants, which also generate evidence, recover about 3-4pp of that loss and land in the 0.45-0.46 range. The CED reranker gets 0.4864, nearly matching the no-evidence performance. So the pattern is clear: evidence generation carries a significant cost, RSFT helps recover some of it, and CED nearly closes the gap entirely.

Now I'm checking the resource allocation for the report. I have 4 GPUs all running different evaluation jobs, but the worker pool is currently empty since the analysis worker finished and I haven't spawned any others.

I should start workers for the pending tasks like literature linkage and preparing the remaining experiments, but I'll prioritize sending the Director report first since that's the most critical item. Let me also check the literature_map.yaml to see what linkage work needs to be done.

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513_6/projects/faith-evidence-ie/literature_map.yaml\", \"content\": \"# Literature Map for faith-evidence-ie (FREIGE)\\n# Focus: CED-as-training-stabilizer (D024) + RSFT for DocRE\\n# Updated: 2026-05-16 (W4 expanded: +11 papers from literature scan; +15 papers from Related Work survey; +17 papers from Related Work expansion; +16 papers from Related Work extension scan; +15 papers from 2026-05-16 incremental scan; +9 papers from Related Work targeted expansion)\\n\\npapers:\\n\\n  # === Category 1: Reward Diversity / Reward Shaping for RL Stability ===\\n\\n  - title: \\\"GDPO: Group reward-Decoupled Normalization Policy Optimization for Multi-reward RL Optimization\\\"\\n    authors: NVIDIA Labs\\n    year: 2025\\n    venue: ICML 2026\\n    url: https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.05242\\n    category: reward_diversity\\n    role: closest_prior_work\\n    key_finding: >\\n      直接将 GRPO 应用于多 reward 组合时，不同 rollout 的 reward 组合在归一化后 collapse 为相同 advantage 值，\\n      降低训练信号分辨率导致收敛失败。GDPO 对每个 reward 独立做 group-wise normalization 再聚合，\\n      保留多 reward 的相对差异，显著提升训练稳定性。\\n    relevance_to_ced_stabilizer: >\\n      最直接相关。CED reward 本质上引入了独立于 extraction F1 的 reward 维度（evidence quality），\\n      GDPO 的理论分析解释了为什么多维 reward 比单一 reward 更稳定——独立 reward 维度防止 advantage collapse。\\n      但 GDPO 是方法论贡献，CED 是 task-specific reward 设计自然具备了这一特性。\\n\\n  - title: \\\"DRA-GRPO: Exploring Diversity-Aware Reward Adjustment for R1-Zero-Like Training of LLMs\\\"\\n    authors: Xiwen Chen et al.\\n    year: 2025\\n    venue: arXiv 2505.09655\\n    url: https://arxiv.org/abs/2505.09655\\n    category: reward_diversity\\n    role: related_method\\n    key_finding: >\\n      GRPO 的标量 reward 无法区分语义多样但得分相同的输出（diversity-quality inconsistency）。\\n      DRA 用子模互信息（SMI）对 reward 加权，给多样性高的 completion 更大权重，\\n      等价于 Inverse Propensity Scoring 去偏，防止 policy 坍缩到先验模式。\\n    relevance_to_ced_stabilizer: >\\n      DRA 通过后处理 reward 注入多样性信号；CED 的 reward 本身就内含对比结构自然提供 diversity 信号。\\n      机制互补但来源不同——CED 是 reward design 层面，DRA 是 optimization 层面。\\n\\n  - title: \\\"Shrinking the Variance: Shrinkage Baselines for RL with Verifiable Rewards\\\"\\n    authors: Guanning Zeng et al.\\n    year: 2025\\n    venue: arXiv 2511.03710\\n    url: https://arxiv.org/abs/2511.03710\\n    category: reward_diversity\\n    role: theoretical_support\\n    key_finding: >\\n      RLVR 中 per-prompt 均值 baseline 在低 generation 数下估计不准导致高 variance 梯度。\\n      用 James-Stein shrinkage 混合 per-prompt 和 cross-prompt 均值，\\n      构造可证明更低 variance 的 policy gradient estimator。\\n    relevance_to_ced_stabilizer: >\\n      理论支持：reward variance 对 gradient estimation 质量至关重要。\\n      CED 的额外 reward 维度提供更丰富的 per-group variance，间接起到 variance 维持效果。\\n\\n  - title: \\\"Reward Shaping to Mitigate Reward Hacking in RLHF\\\"\\n    authors: Anonymous\\n    year: 2025\\n    venue: arXiv 2502.18770\\n    url: https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.18770\\n    category: reward_diversity\\n    role: background\\n    key_finding: >\\n      RL reward 应有界且先快速增长后缓慢收敛。系统研究 reward shaping 原则，\\n      好的 reward 形状能抑制 reward hacking 并提升训练稳定性。\\n    relevance_to_ced_stabilizer: >\\n      CED reward 的 0-1 对比判别结构天然满足有界性，\\n      且 evidence quality 的渐进提升符合\\\"先快后慢\\\"的 reward shaping 原则。\\n\\n  # === Category 2: Mode Collapse Prevention in GRPO/RLHF ===\\n\\n  - title: \\\"DAPO: An Open-Source LLM Reinforcement Learning System at Scale\\\"\\n    authors: ByteDance Seed, Tsinghua AIR\\n    year: 2025\\n    venue: NeurIPS 2025\\n    url: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14476\\n    category: mode_collapse_prevention\\n    role: key_baseline\\n    key_finding: >\\n      Naive GRPO 存在 entropy collapse（policy 熵快速下降→输出同质化）。\\n      根因：clipping 不对称性——概率 0.01 的 token 最多增到 0.012，而概率 0.9 的 token 可达 1.08，\\n      低概率 token 难以增长，抑制探索。DAPO 提出 Clip-Higher（εhigh=0.28 > εlow=0.2）\\n      为低概率 token 留更大增长空间，加 Dynamic Sampling 过滤 accuracy=0 或 1 的 prompt\\n      （这些 prompt 产生零梯度，降低有效 batch size）。在 AIME 2024 达 50 分超越 DeepSeek-R1-Zero。\\n    relevance_to_ced_stabilizer: >\\n      DAPO 从 optimization 技巧角度解决 entropy collapse。CED reward 从 reward design 层面\\n      防止 mode collapse——evidence quality 维度确保 reward 组内有足够 variance，\\n      不会出现 all-same-reward 的零梯度 prompt。DAPO 的 Dynamic Sampling 过滤 accuracy=0/1 prompt\\n      与我们观察到的 frac_zero_std→0.8 现象本质相同：extraction-only reward 下大量 prompt\\n      产生零 advantage，CED 从源头（reward 设计）而非后处理（过滤）解决这个问题。\\n\\n  - title: \\\"Dr. GRPO: Removing Optimization Bias in GRPO\\\"\\n    authors: Liu et al.\\n    year: 2025\\n    venue: COLM 2025\\n    url: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20783\\n    category: mode_collapse_prevention\\n    role: related_analysis\\n    key_finding: >\\n      GRPO 的 std 归一化引入两个 bias：(1) difficulty bias——std(r) 归一化对低 variance 组\\n      （全对/全错）给予过高 advantage 权重，trivial question 主导训练信号；\\n      (2) length bias——per-token loss 的 1/|o_i| 项使长的错误回答被 under-penalize。\\n      当 std(r)→0 时 advantage 爆炸或未定义，梯度数值不稳定。\\n      Dr. GRPO 移除 std 归一化，用无偏 Monte Carlo advantage：Ã = R(q,o_i) - mean(R)。\\n    relevance_to_ced_stabilizer: >\\n      与我们的实验发现高度一致：extraction-only reward 下 frac_zero_std→0.8 正是\\n      Dr. GRPO 分析的 difficulty bias 极端情况——大量 prompt 的所有 rollout 得到相同 F1，\\n      std→0 导致 advantage 异常。CED 通过增加 reward 维度天然维持 reward_std>0，\\n      从 reward 设计而非算法修改角度避免了 Dr. GRPO 发现的 bias。\\n\\n  - title: \\\"GVPO: Group Variance Policy Optimization for LLM Post-Training\\\"\\n    authors: Anonymous\\n    year: 2025\\n    venue: NeurIPS 2025\\n    url: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19599\\n    category: mode_collapse_prevention\\n    role: alternative_framework\\n    key_finding: >\\n      将 KL 约束 reward 最大化的解析解直接嵌入梯度权重，\\n      variance 项自然平衡 exploration 与 exploitation，维持适度 entropy。\\n    relevance_to_ced_stabilizer: >\\n      GVPO 的 variance 项机制与 CED 维持 reward variance 的效果功能类似，\\n      但 GVPO 是算法层面 implicit regularization，CED 是 reward 层面 explicit structure。\\n\\n  - title: \\\"REINFORCE++: Stabilizing Critic-Free Policy Optimization with Global Advantage Normalization\\\"\\n    authors: Jian Hu et al.\\n    year: 2025\\n    venue: arXiv 2501.03262\\n    url: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.03262\\n    category: mode_collapse_prevention\\n    role: related_method\\n    key_finding: >\\n      Prompt-level (local) advantage normalization 导致不准确估计和过拟合。\\n      Global batch normalization 提供更稳定、理论无偏的 gradient estimator。\\n    relevance_to_ced_stabilizer: >\\n      解决 local normalization 的 variance 问题；CED 从 rewar\\n\\n... title: \\\"DAPO: An Open-Source LLM Reinforcement Learning System at Scale\\\"\\n    authors: ByteDance Seed, Tsinghua AIR\\n    year: 2025\\n    venue: NeurIPS 2025\\n    url: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.14476\\n    category: mode_collapse_prevention\\n    role: key_baseline\\n    key_finding: >\\n      Naive GRPO 存在 entropy collapse（policy 熵快速下降→输出同质化）。\\n      根因：clipping 不对称性——概率 0.01 的 token 最多增到 0.012，而概率 0.9 的 token 可达 1.08，\\n      低概率 token 难以增长，抑制探索。DAPO 提出 Clip-Higher（εhigh=0.28 > εlow=0.2）\\n      为低概率 token 留更大增长空间，加 Dynamic Sampling 过滤 accuracy=0 或 1 的 prompt\\n      （这些 prompt 产生零梯度，降低有效 batch size）。在 AIME 2024 达 50 分超越 DeepSeek-R1-Zero。\\n    relevance_to_ced_stabilizer: >\\n      DAPO 从 optimization 技巧角度解决 entropy collapse。CED reward 从 reward design 层面\\n      防止 mode collapse——evidence quality 维度确保 reward 组内有足够 variance，\\n      不会出现 all-same-reward 的零梯度 prompt。DAPO 的 Dynamic Sampling 过滤 accuracy=0/1 prompt\\n      与我们观察到的 frac_zero_std→0.8 现象本质相同：extraction-only reward 下大量 prompt\\n      产生零 advantage，CED 从源头（reward 设计）而非后处理（过滤）解决这个问题。\\n\\n  - title: \\\"Dr. GRPO: Removing Optimization Bias in GRPO\\\"\\n    authors: Liu et al.\\n    year: 2025\\n    venue: COLM 2025\\n    url: https://arxiv.org/abs/2503.20783\\n    category: mode_collapse_prevention\\n    role: related_analysis\\n    key_finding: >\\n      GRPO 的 std 归一化引入两个 bias：(1) difficulty bias——std(r) 归一化对低 variance 组\\n      （全对/全错）给予过高 advantage 权重，trivial question 主导训练信号；\\n      (2) length bias——per-token loss 的 1/|o_i| 项使长的错误回答被 under-penalize。\\n      当 std(r)→0 时 advantage 爆炸或未定义，梯度数值不稳定。\\n      Dr. GRPO 移除 std 归一化，用无偏 Monte Carlo advantage：Ã = R(q,o_i) - mean(R)。\\n    relevance_to_ced_stabilizer: >\\n      与我们的实验发现高度一致：extraction-only reward 下 frac_zero_std→0.8 正是\\n      Dr. GRPO 分析的 difficulty bias 极端情况——大量 prompt 的所有 rollout 得到相同 F1，\\n      std→0 导致 advantage 异常。CED 通过增加 reward 维度天然维持 reward_std>0，\\n      从 reward 设计而非算法修改角度避免了 Dr. GRPO 发现的 bias。\\n\\n  - title: \\\"GVPO: Group Variance Policy Optimization for LLM Post-Training\\\"\\n    authors: Anonymous\\n    year: 2025\\n    venue: NeurIPS 2025\\n    url: https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.19599\\n    category: mode_collapse_prevention\\n    role: alternative_framework\\n    key_finding: >\\n      将 KL 约束 reward 最大化的解析解直接嵌入梯度权重，\\n      variance 项自然平衡 exploration 与 exploitation，维持适度 entropy。\\n    relevance_to_ced_stabilizer: >\\n      GVPO 的 variance 项机制与 CED 维持 reward variance 的效果功能类似，\\n      但 GVPO 是算法层面 implicit regularization，CED 是 reward 层面 explicit structure。\\n\\n  - title: \\\"REINFORCE++: Stabilizing Critic-Free Policy Optimization with Global Advantage Normalization\\\"\\n    authors: Jian Hu et al.\\n    year: 2025\\n    venue: arXiv 2501.03262\\n    url: https://arxiv.org/abs/2501.03262\\n    category: mode_collapse_prevention\\n    role: related_method\\n    key_finding: >\\n      Prompt-level (local) advantage normalization 导致不准确估计和过拟合。\\n      Global batch normalization 提供更稳定、理论无偏的 gradient estimator。\\n    relevance_to_ced_stabilizer: >\\n      解决 local normalization 的 variance 问题；CED 从 rewar\\n\\n... {
  "task_type": "research:evidence-cost-literature",
  "project": "faith-evidence-ie",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务\n搜索与 \"evidence generation cost in information extraction\" 相关的文献。\n\n## 背景\n我们的项目 (FREIGE) 发现在 DocRE（文档级关系抽取）中，让模型同时生成 evidence 和关系会导致 rel_f1 下降约 7.7 个百分点（no-evidence SFT 0.4876 vs evidence-SFT 0.4106）。这是 \"faithfulness 的真实成本\"。\n\n目前 literature_map.yaml 中没有直接讨论这一现象的论文。我们需要确认这是否是新颖贡献，并找到可以引用的相关工作。\n\n## 搜索关键词\n1. \"cost of faithfulness\" + information extraction\n2. \"evidence generation\" + \"relation extraction\" + performance\n3. \"joint evidence and relation extraction\" + trade-off\n4. \"faithful information extraction\" + cost / overhead\n5. \"explanation generation\" + IE + \"performance degradation\"\n6. \"rationale generation\" + NLP + \"task performance\"\n7. \"chain-of-thought\" + extraction + \"accuracy trade-off\"\n\n## 你要做的\n1. 用 WebSearch 搜索上述关键词（每个 1-2 次搜索）\n2. 找到讨论以下任一主题的论文：\n   - 生成 evidence/rationale/explanation 对主任务性能的影响\n   - Joint evidence+relation extraction 的 trade-off\n   - Faithful IE 的性能代价\n3. 对每篇相关论文记录：title, authors, year, venue, url, key_finding, relevance\n4. 特别关注是否有人量化过 \"evidence tax\"（即加 evidence 后性能下降多少）\n5. 用 save_project_file 将结果追加到 literature_map.yaml 的合适 category 中\n\n## 输出\n- 如果找到直接竞品：详细记录，标记 role=direct_competitor\n- 如果只找到间接相关：记录为 background/related_method\n- 如果确认无人做过类似量化：明确说明，这强化我们的新颖性\n- 将搜索结果总结写入 artifacts/evidence_cost_literature_scan.md",
  "tool_groups": [
    "knowledge"
  ],
  "tools": [
    "WebSearch"
  ]
}
